 5. C.  08.21.18 
5. CURRICULUM 
C. CONSIDERATION:   Schools of Choice - Section 105c 
     9th Grade for 2019 – 2020 
In accordance with Michigan  state law, school districts  shall pass a resolution 
each school year if they intend to participate in a Schools of Choice program.  It 
is anticipated that seats will be available for the 2019-2020 school year for grade 
9 at the International Academy East.  It would be appropriate at this time to 
consider adopting a Schools of Choice section 105c resolution for 85 non-resident 
9th grade students  who reside outside of Oakland County , if that intermediate 
school district is contiguous with the Oakland Intermediate School District 
boundaries.   
RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Adopt the following resolution. 
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
 Moved by: __________________________ 
 Supported by: __________________________ 
Be it therefore resolved that the Board of Education of the Troy School 
District, exercising the option permitted by Section 105c  of the State School Aid 
Act of 1979, as amended by Public Act 300 of 1996, will accept applications for a 
Schools of Choice program for the 2019-2020 school year for 85 non-resident 9th 
grade students who reside outside of Oakland  County, if that Intermediate 
School District is contiguous wi th the Oakland Intermediate School District 
boundaries for the purposes of attending the International Academy East 
program located in the Troy School District. 
Yes: __________ 
No: __________

 8. A. 08.21.18 
8. BUSINESS 
A.  CONSIDERATION:  CONTRACT ADDENDUM APPROVAL FOR 
CUSTODIAL SERVICES 
Below is a resolution for Board consideration regarding negotiating an 
addendum to the Custodial, Grounds and Warehouse Services Contract 
with D.M. Burr Facilities Management, Inc. to reflect the increased 
wages necessary to compete in the current labor market. 
 RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Accept the resolution below. 
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
 Moved by:   
 Supported by: 
 Be it therefore resolved that the Troy Board of Education authorize 
administration to proceed with finalizing an addendum to the contract with 
D.M. Burr  Facilities Management, Inc. which will increase the aggregate 
contract price to reflect a minimum wage of $12.00 per hour for custodians.  
The base contract increase shall not exceed $364,000 and this will be an 
approved expenditure of the General Fund. 
 Yes: 
 No: 
168
Troy School District
